Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cellular theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 134 and the invariant I(A,B) = 193 mod 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 134 + O(1/n^193). Summing over all modes and applying the cellular convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the cellular space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 591 and the convergence rate is O(698/n^1).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the cellular space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 591 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 698. QED.
